I found the typo in the `config.yaml` file.

**The Issue:**
The database host is misspelled as `localhsot` instead of `localhost`. The letters 'h' and 's' are swapped.

**Current (incorrect):**
```yaml
db:
  host: localhsot
```

**The Fix:**
Change `localhsot` to `localhost`:
```yaml
db:
  host: localhost
```

This is a common typo where the letters in "localhost" get transposed. The correct spelling is `localhost` (l-o-c-a-l-h-o-s-t).

verdict=pass
summary=Read the file first, correctly identified 'localhsot' → 'localhost'.
